1/2 cup chopped green bell       Add onion, green pepper, celery, and garlic; cook until tender.
pepper

2 cloves garlic, minced          Worcestershire sauce, chili powder, and red pepper sauce. Bring to
3 tablespoons butter             a boil, stirring frequently. Stir in shrimp, and cook for 5 minutes.
2 tablespoons cornstarch
1 (14.5 ounce) can stewed
tomatoes
1 (8 ounce) can tomato sauce
1 tablespoon Worcestershire
sauce

1 dash hot pepper sauce

and deveined
